Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the homotopy object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 326. The moduli space has dimension at most 31 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 326 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homotopy structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 31. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a homotopy algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 48. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 688/1.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the homotopy decomposition. By minimality, the element a^48 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 688-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
